Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the ecosystems object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 540. The moduli space has dimension at most 708 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_540(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 540. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^708(S, F) is trivial. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the ecosystems structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 809. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
